956 entries, sap-va01 to sca-review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sap-va01**: Manages SAP sales orders via VA01/VA02/VA03 using SAP GUI Scripting. Creates new sales orders or updates existing ones. Existence check (VA03 Display), order creation (VA01) with header/item handling, order update (VA02), and save. Field values are provided as tab-separated section/field/value triples in a definition file. By default an order that SAP reports as INCOMPLETE at save time is NOT saved (fail-loud); pass --allow-incomplete to save it anyway. Prerequisites: Active SAP GUI session (use /sap-login first). ([sapdev-ai/sap-dev](https://github.com/sapdev-ai/sap-dev/tree/HEAD/plugins/sap-project/skills/sap-va01) · ★8)
